# Generated UI hierarchy and interactions

Generated by `ui::hierarchy::implementation_hierarchy_matches_reviewed_map` from actual Screen controls and App events. Do not edit this file by hand.

This is a bounded generated-fixture map, not proof of exhaustive UI or good UX. Every Screen implementation must expose its actual control in test builds. Toasts and pending app requests are observed, widget events use actual handlers, and Book Info metadata comes from its rendered lines. Generated account namespace hashes are omitted only from display; duplicate checks retain complete scoped identities. Same-page duplicate destinations and stable row keys are checked independently of snapshot approval.

Coverage: generated ABS production-client/cache roots with chooser and separate book/podcast defaults; unconfigured/configured-offline roots, music stars/hearts/both, book, podcast, live radio and unavailable mainline stock handover; Home and local menus, generated music cache album/artist/playlist/genre routes, paused queue, Settings choices/confirmations/editors reachable within six edges, actual long-Center menus and Now Playing Center cycles, and observed Back targets. Repeated identical non-contextual surfaces are emitted once per capability context; context menus, playback modes, speed and info controls remain separate for every media/rating fixture. Script control readiness means an initial control is settled (including stable errors), not that a transport is connected. Large data catalogs retain their row count and complete label/key fingerprint, with three representative rows expanded.

Limits: live online transport/catalog pagination, network/USB hotplug, signed-in Tailscale/Wi-Fi scan states, actual system information, stored/offline media and error/retry interleavings are not enumerated here. Generated cached ABS lists and metadata are populated; genuinely empty lists are explicitly marked. Background page jobs are disabled: download taps expose their immediate loading/queueing state, while asynchronous completion/removal states require separate fixtures. Accepted library-choice writes settle through the real ordered settings owner before mapping their durable destination; pending storage, newer edits and Back during a save have separate controls. Timing, audio correctness, physical input feel, visuals and discoverability need separate tests and UX review.

Source includes Center-speed controls.

## Actual unconfigured startup

App::new initially opens `Navidrome Server` with actual rows: Address / User / Password / Test Connection / Scan Library / Full Sync / Or edit config.toml. Back → `Q2`; the Home traces below begin after this deliberate return.

## Conditional Home spoken availability

Generated worker completion values are delivered through the production Home completion handler; configured offline setup is independent of reachability. Actual generated M4B/download discovery, missing-file/catalog negatives and Home/Back selection transitions have separate App worker tests. Unknown card/index reads retain known rows. These probes cover Home visibility only, not populated local-content playback.

| ABS setup | Disk worker evidence | Actual Home rows |
| --- | --- | --- |
| unconfigured | neither | Set Up Server / Music / Radios / Downloads / Settings / Shuffle Songs |
| unconfigured | books only | Set Up Server / Music / Books / Radios / Downloads / Settings / Shuffle Songs |
| unconfigured | podcasts only | Set Up Server / Music / Podcasts / Radios / Downloads / Settings / Shuffle Songs |
| unconfigured | both | Set Up Server / Music / Books / Podcasts / Radios / Downloads / Settings / Shuffle Songs |
| configured | neither / offline | Set Up Server / Music / Books / Podcasts / Radios / Downloads / Settings / Shuffle Songs |

## Generated storage accounting contexts

These fixtures deliver generated counts through the actual asynchronous completion handler, then use App wheel/Back events. Filesystem allocation/inode accounting has independent generated-file tests.

### Storage: mixed cache/music-spoken downloads/other/reserved/free

Clockwise detents: `wedges=Downloads,Song Cache,Other Files,Reserved Space,Free;selected=0 → wedges=Downloads,Song Cache,Other Files,Reserved Space,Free;selected=1 → wedges=Downloads,Song Cache,Other Files,Reserved Space,Free;selected=2 → wedges=Downloads,Song Cache,Other Files,Reserved Space,Free;selected=3 → wedges=Downloads,Song Cache,Other Files,Reserved Space,Free;selected=4 → wedges=Downloads,Song Cache,Other Files,Reserved Space,Free;selected=0`.

Center on Downloads / Song Cache / Other Files opens `Downloaded Items / Song Cache / Media Folders`. Back keeps each wedge selected. Populated rows and long-Center actions have separate fixture tests.

Back → `Storage`; Center reopens `Storage Usage` at the retained parent row.

### Storage: empty filesystem

Clockwise detents: `wedges=Free;selected=0 → wedges=Free;selected=0`.

Back → `Storage`; Center reopens `Storage Usage` at the retained parent row.

### Storage: full filesystem

Clockwise detents: `wedges=Other Files;selected=0 → wedges=Other Files;selected=0`.

Back → `Storage`; Center reopens `Storage Usage` at the retained parent row.

### Storage: zero capacity

Clockwise detents: `wedges=;selected=0 → wedges=;selected=0`.

Back → `Storage`; Center reopens `Storage Usage` at the retained parent row.

### Storage: missing storage

Clockwise detents: `unavailable → unavailable`.

Back → `Storage`; Center reopens `Storage Usage` at the retained parent row.

### Storage: inconsistent concurrent counts

Clockwise detents: `unavailable → unavailable`.

Back → `Storage`; Center reopens `Storage Usage` at the retained parent row.

## Required critical journeys

These labels are requirement-oriented fixture inputs; targets and action counts come from actual runtime rows and events. Wheel repositioning is fixture setup, not an estimate of human wheel travel.

| Journey from Q2 | Center choices | Actual target | Back | Center after Back |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Settings → Playback → Default Spoken Speed | 3 | Default Speed | Playback | Default Speed |
| Settings → Playback → Skip Forward | 3 | Skip Forward | Playback | Skip Forward |
| Settings → Playback → Skip Backward | 3 | Skip Backward | Playback | Skip Backward |
| Settings → Storage → Storage Usage | 3 | Storage Usage | Storage | Storage Usage |

With Offline Mix enabled, Storage shows **Mix Size**. Its initial selection is **50% of card**. Back returns to the same Storage row.

## Required spoken selection journeys

Generated ABS records are parsed by the production client and cache. These offline map probes disable workers: Opening is a pending operation, not proof of a playback session. Async success, cancellation and retained resume have separate production-handler tests.

| Home journey | Center choices to initial target | Initial control | One more Center | Back | Retained row |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Books → All Books → Generated Book | 3 | Book Info / BookInfoScreen | Generated Book / Opening (pending) | All Books | Generated Book |
| Podcasts → Latest Episodes → Generated Episode | 3 | Generated Episode / Opening | Already Opening (direct episode play) | Latest Episodes | Generated Episode |

## Wi-Fi sign-in and Delta account preparation

Generated lease and portal states drive the actual Wi-Fi screen and wheel controls. These probes cover the conditional controls, their immediate actions and Back. They do not connect a phone, reach a live provider or check media throughput.

| Flow | Observed control | Wheel, Center or Back result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Delta account prep without an address | Start Phone Setup → Wi-Fi QR | Center shows Setup QR; Center returns to the Wi-Fi QR; Back cancels and returns to Wi-Fi |
| Account received | Account Ready | Done returns to Wi-Fi and retains the prepared account |
| Guest agreement | Wi-Fi Sign-In → Terms & Conditions apply / Agree | Agree is selected immediately. Long Center leaves the prompt open. Back cancels and returns to Wi-Fi |
| Guest agreement acceptance | Agree | Unverified access offers Retry. Verified access shows Internet Available |
| Delta phone entry | Enter Details on Phone / Enter SkyMiles Details | Back cancels setup. A fresh check restores the Delta choices on the same network |
| Account received during Delta sign-in | Done | Rechecks the current link. The prepared account stays available, and its sign-in action appears only after a Delta check |
| Delta direct sign-in | SkyMiles Account → SkyMiles Password | The helper checks first. Only its credentials request opens the wheel editor. Return cancels and rechecks Delta Wi-Fi |
| Delta prepared-account sign-in | Use Prepared Account | Center rechecks a newly published lease without consuming the account. After Delta is verified, Center begins sign-in. Back before the password prompt returns to Wi-Fi and keeps the account ready |
